<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 148E. BOARD OF SOCIAL WORK PRACTICE</h2>
      
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.001">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.001 CITATION.</h1>
        <p class="in">This chapter may be cited as the "Minnesota Board of Social Work Practice Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.001.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.63.0">2007 c 123 s 63</a>,138</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.010">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.010 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For the purpose of this chapter, the terms in this section have the meanings given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Applicant.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Applicant" means a person who submits an application to the board for a new license, a license renewal, a change in license, an inactive license, reactivation of a license, or a voluntary termination.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Application" means an application to the board for a new license, a license renewal, a change in license, an inactive license, reactivation of a license, or voluntary termination.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Baccalaureate degree.</span></h2>
          <p>"Baccalaureate degree" means a bachelor's degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Board.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Board" means the Board of Social Work created under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.025">148E.025</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Client.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Client" means an individual, couple, family, group, community, or organization that receives or has received social work services as described in subdivision 11.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Client system.</span></h2>
          <p>"Client system" means the client and those in the client's environment who are potentially influential in contributing to a resolution of the client's issues.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Clinical practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Clinical practice" means applying professional social work knowledge, skills, and values in the differential diagnosis and treatment of psychosocial function, disability, or impairment, including addictions and emotional, mental, and behavioral disorders. Treatment includes a plan based on a differential diagnosis. Treatment may include, but is not limited to, the provision of psychotherapy to individuals, couples, families, and groups across the life span. Clinical social workers may also provide the services described in subdivision 11.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Clinical supervision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Clinical supervision" means supervision as defined in subdivision 18 of a social worker engaged in clinical practice as defined in subdivision 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Direct clinical client contact.</span></h2>
          <p>"Direct clinical client contact" means in-person or electronic media interaction with a client, including client systems and service providers, related to the client's mental and emotional functioning, differential diagnosis, and treatment, in subdivision 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7b.<span class="headnote">Clock hour.</span></h2>
          <p>"Clock hour" means 60 minutes of learning.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7c.<span class="headnote">Continuing education.</span></h2>
          <p>"Continuing education" means education and training to maintain, improve, or enhance competent social work practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7d.<span class="headnote">Continuing education independent learning.</span></h2>
          <p>"Continuing education independent learning" means a licensee's individual study, including but not limited to online activities without live interaction, research for publication, presentations, or professional development.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7e.<span class="headnote">Continuing education social work ethics.</span></h2>
          <p>"Continuing education social work ethics" means:</p>
          <p>(1) the history and evolution of values and ethics in social work, including cultural awareness and social diversity;</p>
          <p>(2) ethics theories;</p>
          <p>(3) professional standards of social work practice, as specified in the ethical codes of the National Association of Social Workers, the Association of Canadian Social Workers, the Clinical Social Work Federation, and the Council on Social Work Education;</p>
          <p>(4) the legal requirements and other considerations for each jurisdiction that registers, certifies, or licenses social workers; or</p>
          <p>(5) the ethical decision-making process.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.7f">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7f.<span class="headnote">Cultural responsiveness.</span></h2>
          <p>"Cultural responsiveness" means increasing the knowledge, understanding, self-awareness, and practice skills that enable a social worker to serve clients from diverse socioeconomic and cultural backgrounds including:</p>
          <p>(1) understanding culture, its functions, and strengths that exist in varied cultures;</p>
          <p>(2) understanding clients' cultures and differences among and between cultural groups;</p>
          <p>(3) understanding the nature of social diversity and oppression; and</p>
          <p>(4) understanding cultural humility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Graduate degree.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Graduate degree" means a master's degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body designated by the board; or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Intern.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Intern" means a student in field placement working under the supervision of a social worker to fulfill the requirements of a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Person-in-environment perspective.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Person-in-environment perspective" means viewing human behavior, development, and function in the context of one or more of the following: the environment, social functioning, mental health, and physical health.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Practice of social work.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) "Practice of social work" means working to maintain, restore, or improve behavioral, cognitive, emotional, mental, or social functioning of clients, in a manner that applies accepted professional social work knowledge, skills, and values, including the person-in-environment perspective, by providing in person or through telephone, video conferencing, or electronic means one or more of the social work services described in paragraph (b), clauses (1) to (3). Social work services may address conditions that impair or limit behavioral, cognitive, emotional, mental, or social functioning. Such conditions include, but are not limited to, the following: abuse and neglect of children or vulnerable adults, addictions, developmental disorders, disabilities, discrimination, illness, injuries, poverty, and trauma. Practice of social work also means providing social work services in a position for which the educational basis is the individual's degree in social work described in subdivision 13.</p>
          <p>(b) Social work services include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) providing assessment and intervention through direct contact with clients, developing a plan based on information from an assessment, and providing services which include, but are not limited to, assessment, case management, client-centered advocacy, client education, consultation, counseling, crisis intervention, and referral;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) providing for the direct or indirect benefit of clients through administrative, educational, policy, or research services including, but not limited to:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) advocating for policies, programs, or services to improve the well-being of clients;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) conducting research related to social work services;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) developing and administering programs which provide social work services;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) engaging in community organization to address social problems and macrocosm issues through planned collective action;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) supervising individuals who provide social work services to clients;</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) supervising social workers in order to comply with the supervised practice requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vii) teaching professional social work knowledge, skills, and values to students; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) engaging in clinical practice as defined in subdivision 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Professional name.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Professional name" means the name a licensed social worker uses in making representations of the social worker's professional status to the public and which has been designated to the board in writing according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.090">148E.090</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Professional social work knowledge, skills, and values.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Professional social work knowledge, skills, and values" means the knowledge, skills, and values taught in programs accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body designated by the board; or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university. Professional social work knowledge, skills, and values include, but are not limited to, principles of person-in-environment and the values, principles, and standards described in the Code of Ethics of the National Association of Social Workers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Sexual conduct.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Sexual conduct" means any physical contact or conduct that may be reasonably interpreted as sexual, or any oral, written, electronic, or other communication that suggests engaging in physical contact or conduct that may be reasonably interpreted as sexual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Social worker" means an individual who:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) is licensed as a social worker; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has obtained a social work degree from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body designated by the board and engages in the practice of social work.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Student.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Student" means an individual who is currently enrolled in a program that has been accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates and who studies professional social work knowledge, skills, and values.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Supervisee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Supervisee" means an individual who receives evaluation and supervision or direction of the supervisee's social work practice through supervision in order to comply with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a> by an individual who meets the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Supervision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Supervision" means a professional relationship between a supervisor and a supervisee in which the supervisor evaluates and directs the supervisee's social work practice. A supervisor's role is to promote a supervisee's continued development of the supervisee's knowledge, skills, and abilities to practice social work in an ethical and competent manner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Supervisor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Supervisor" means an individual who meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a> to provide evaluation and direction through supervision as described in subdivision 18 in order to comply with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.010.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Underrepresented community.</span></h2>
          <p>"Underrepresented community" means a group that is not represented in the majority with respect to race, ethnicity, national origin, sexual orientation, gender identity, or physical ability.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.010.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.64.0">2007 c 123 s 64</a>,138; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.1.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 1</a>-5,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.1.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 1</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.4.12.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 4 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.015">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.015 SCOPE.</h1>
        <p class="in">This chapter applies to all applicants and licensees, all persons who use the title social worker, and all persons who engage in the practice of social work, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>, in this state, regardless of the practitioner's or client's location, unless there are specific applicable exemptions provided by law.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.015.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.65.0">2007 c 123 s 65</a>,138; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.13.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 13</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.020">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.020 CHAPTER 214.</h1>
        <p class="in">Chapter 214 applies to the Board of Social Work unless superseded by this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.020.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.66.0">2007 c 123 s 66</a>,138</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.025">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.025 BOARD OF SOCIAL WORK.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.025.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Creation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The Board of Social Work consists of 15 members appointed by the governor. The members are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) ten social workers licensed according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) five public members as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.02">214.02</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.025.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Qualifications of board members.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) All social worker members must have engaged in the practice of social work in Minnesota for at least one year during the ten years preceding their appointments.</p>
          <p>(b) The ten social worker members must include at least:</p>
          <p>(1) three licensed social workers;</p>
          <p>(2) two licensed independent clinical social workers; and</p>
          <p>(3) two licensed graduate social workers or licensed independent social workers.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Five social worker members must be engaged at the time of their appointment in the practice of social work in Minnesota in the following settings:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) one member must be engaged in the practice of social work in either a county or state agency;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) one member must be engaged in the practice of social work in an elementary, middle, or secondary school;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) one member must be engaged in the practice of social work in a health care setting;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) one member must be engaged in the practice of social work in a private setting; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) one member must be an educator engaged in regular teaching duties at a program of social work accredited by the Council on Social Work Education or a similar accreditation body that the board designates.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) At the time of their appointments, at least six members must reside outside of the 11-county metropolitan area.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) At the time of their appointments, at least five members must be members of:</p>
          <p>(1) a community of color; or</p>
          <p>(2) an underrepresented community, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010#stat.148E.010.20">148E.010, subdivision 20</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.025.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Officers.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board must biennially elect from its membership a chair, vice-chair, and secretary-treasurer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.025.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board must adopt bylaws to govern its proceedings.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.025.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Executive director.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board must appoint and employ an executive director who is not a member of the board. The employment of the executive director shall be subject to the terms described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.04#stat.214.04.2a">214.04, subdivision 2a</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.025.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.67.0">2007 c 123 s 67</a>,138; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.6.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 6</a>,7,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.14.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.030">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.030 DUTIES OF THE BOARD.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.030.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Duties.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board must perform the duties necessary to promote and protect the public health, safety, and welfare through the licensure and regulation of persons who practice social work in this state. These duties include, but are not limited to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) establishing the qualifications and procedures for individuals to be licensed as social workers;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) establishing standards of practice for social workers;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) holding examinations or contracting with the Association of Social Work Boards or a similar examination body designated by the board to hold examinations to assess applicants' qualifications;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) issuing licenses to qualified individuals according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.060">148E.060</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) taking disciplinary, adversarial, corrective, or other action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> when an individual violates the requirements of this chapter;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) assessing fees according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.175">148E.175</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) educating social workers and the public on the requirements of the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.030.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board may adopt and enforce rules to carry out the duties specified in subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.030.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.68.0">2007 c 123 s 68</a>,138</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.035">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.035 VARIANCES.</h1>
        <p class="in">If the effect of a requirement according to this chapter is unreasonable, impossible to execute, absurd, or would impose an extreme hardship on a licensee, the board may grant a variance if the variance is consistent with promoting and protecting the public health, safety, and welfare. A variance must not be granted for core licensing standards such as substantive educational and examination requirements.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.035.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.69.0">2007 c 123 s 69</a>,138</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.040">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.040 IMMUNITY.</h1>
        <p class="in">Board members, board employees, and persons engaged on behalf of the board are immune from civil liability for any actions, transactions, or publications in the lawful execution of or relating to their duties under this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.040.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.70.0">2007 c 123 s 70</a>,138</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.045"><b>148E.045</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.050">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.050 LICENSING; SCOPE OF PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.050.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The practice of social work must comply with the requirements of subdivision 2, 3, 4, or 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.050.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Licensed social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensed social worker may engage in social work practice except that a licensed social worker must not engage in clinical practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.050.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Licensed graduate social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensed graduate social worker may engage in social work practice except that a licensed graduate social worker must not engage in clinical practice except under the supervision of a licensed independent clinical social worker or an alternate supervisor according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.050.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Licensed independent social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensed independent social worker may engage in social work practice except that a licensed independent social worker must not engage in clinical practice except under the supervision of a licensed independent clinical social worker or an alternate supervisor according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.050.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Licensed independent clinical social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensed independent clinical social worker may engage in social work practice, including clinical practice.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.050.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.72.0">2007 c 123 s 72</a>,138</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.055">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.055 LICENSE REQUIREMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License required.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> In order to practice social work, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010, subdivisions 6</a> and 11, an individual must have a social work license under this section or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.060">148E.060</a>, except when the individual is exempt from licensure according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by examination as a licensed social worker (LSW).</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> To be licensed as a licensed social worker, an applicant for licensure by examination must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has received a baccalaureate degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has passed the bachelor's or equivalent examination administered by the Association of Social Work Boards or a similar examination body that the board designates. An examination is not valid if the applicant took and passed the examination eight or more years prior to submitting an application for licensure under this section. An applicant must take the examination no more than six months prior to completing degree requirements;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by endorsement as a licensed social worker (LSW).</span></h2>
          <p>(a) To be licensed as a licensed social worker, an applicant for licensure by endorsement must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) holds an active license or credential as a baccalaureate-level social worker in another jurisdiction;</p>
          <p>(2) holds a bachelor's degree from an accredited college or university;</p>
          <p>(3) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p>(5) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>; and</p>
          <p>(6) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) An individual licensed under this subdivision is not required to meet the supervised practice requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> provided that the applicant has practiced baccalaureate-level social work in another jurisdiction for at least 4,000 hours while actively licensed or credentialed as a baccalaureate-level social worker during the four years immediately preceding the applicant's submission of an application under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements as a licensed social worker (LSW).</span></h2>
          <p>To be licensed as a licensed social worker, an applicant for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) completed all requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0551">148E.0551, subdivisions 1</a> to 6; and</p>
          <p>(2) continues to meet the requirements of subdivision 2, clauses (1) and (3) to (6).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by examination as a licensed graduate social worker (LGSW).</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> To be licensed as a licensed graduate social worker, an applicant for licensure by examination must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has received a graduate degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates, or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has passed the master's or equivalent examination administered by the Association of Social Work Boards or a similar examination body that the board designates. An examination is not valid if the applicant took and passed the examination eight or more years prior to submitting an application for licensure under this section. The applicant must take the examination no more than six months prior to completing degree requirements;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by endorsement as a licensed graduate social worker (LGSW).</span></h2>
          <p>(a) To be licensed as a licensed graduate social worker, an applicant for licensure by endorsement must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) holds an active license or credential as a graduate or master's level social worker in another jurisdiction;</p>
          <p>(2) holds a master's degree from an accredited college or university;</p>
          <p>(3) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p>(5) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>; and</p>
          <p>(6) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) An individual licensed under this subdivision is not required to meet the supervised practice requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.105">148E.105</a> provided that the applicant has practiced nonclinical graduate or master's level social work in another jurisdiction for at least 4,000 hours while actively licensed or credentialed as a graduate or master's level social worker during the four years immediately preceding submission of an application under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(c) An individual licensed under this subdivision engaged in clinical social work practice must comply with the supervised practice requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.106">148E.106</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements as a licensed graduate social worker (LGSW).</span></h2>
          <p>To be licensed as a licensed graduate social worker, an applicant for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) completed all requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0551">148E.0551, subdivisions 1</a> to 6; and</p>
          <p>(2) continues to meet the requirements of subdivision 3, clauses (1) and (3) to (6).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by examination as a licensed independent social worker (LISW).</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> To be licensed as a licensed independent social worker, an applicant for licensure by examination must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has received a graduate degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates, or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has practiced social work as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>, and has met the supervised practice requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has passed the advanced generalist or equivalent examination administered by the Association of Social Work Boards or a similar examination body that the board designates. An examination is not valid if the applicant took and passed the examination eight or more years prior to submitting an application for licensure under this section;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by endorsement as a licensed independent social worker (LISW).</span></h2>
          <p>(a) To be licensed as a licensed independent social worker, an applicant for licensure by endorsement must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) holds an active license or credential as an advanced practice graduate or master's level social worker in another jurisdiction;</p>
          <p>(2) holds a master's degree from an accredited college or university;</p>
          <p>(3) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p>(5) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>; and</p>
          <p>(6) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) An individual licensed under this subdivision engaged in clinical social work practice must comply with the supervised practice requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.110">148E.110</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.4b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements as a licensed independent social worker (LISW).</span></h2>
          <p>To be licensed as a licensed independent social worker, an applicant for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) completed all requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0551">148E.0551, subdivisions 1</a> to 6; and</p>
          <p>(2) continues to meet the requirements of subdivision 4, clauses (1), (2), and (4) to (7).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by examination as a licensed independent clinical social worker (LICSW).</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) To be licensed as a licensed independent clinical social worker, an applicant for licensure by examination must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has received a graduate degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates, or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has completed 360 clock hours (one semester credit hour = 15 clock hours) in the following clinical knowledge areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) 108 clock hours (30 percent) in differential diagnosis and biopsychosocial assessment, including normative development and psychopathology across the life span;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) 36 clock hours (ten percent) in assessment-based clinical treatment planning with measurable goals;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) 108 clock hours (30 percent) in clinical intervention methods informed by research and current standards of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) 18 clock hours (five percent) in evaluation methodologies;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) 72 clock hours (20 percent) in social work values and ethics, including cultural context, diversity, and social policy; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) 18 clock hours (five percent) in culturally specific clinical assessment and intervention;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has practiced clinical social work as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>, including both diagnosis and treatment, and has met the supervised practice requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) has passed the clinical or equivalent examination administered by the Association of Social Work Boards or a similar examination body that the board designates. An examination is not valid if the applicant took and passed the examination eight or more years prior to submitting an application for licensure under this section;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) has paid all applicable license fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(8) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The requirement in paragraph (a), clause (2), may be satisfied through:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a graduate degree program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accreditation body that the board designates; or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university. An academic institution must certify clinical clock hours in the clinical knowledge areas on a form that the board has provided to meet this requirement;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) graduate coursework from an accredited institution of higher learning. An academic institution must certify clinical clock hours in the clinical knowledge areas on a form that the board has provided to meet this requirement; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) up to 120 continuing education hours, which the applicant may complete via continuing education independent learning as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010#stat.148E.010.7d">148E.010, subdivision 7d</a>. The applicant must include documents verifying completion of clinical clock hours in the clinical knowledge areas to meet this requirement as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130#stat.148E.130.11">148E.130, subdivision 11</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by endorsement as a licensed independent clinical social worker (LICSW).</span></h2>
          <p>To be licensed as a licensed independent clinical social worker, an applicant for licensure by endorsement must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) holds an active license or credential as an independent clinical level social worker in another jurisdiction;</p>
          <p>(2) holds a master's degree from an accredited college or university;</p>
          <p>(3) has submitted a completed, signed application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and paid the required fees;</p>
          <p>(5) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>; and</p>
          <p>(6) has paid all applicable fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.5b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5b.<span class="headnote">Qualifications for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements as a licensed independent clinical social worker (LICSW).</span></h2>
          <p>To be licensed as a licensed independent clinical social worker, an applicant for licensure by completion of provisional license requirements must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) completed all requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0551">148E.0551, subdivisions 1</a> to 6; and</p>
          <p>(2) continues to meet the requirements of subdivision 5, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (3) and (5) to (8).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Degrees from outside United States or Canada.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">If an applicant receives a degree from a program outside the United States or Canada that is not accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar examination body designated by the board, the degree does not fulfill the requirements specified in subdivision 2, clause (1); 3, clause (1); 4, clause (1); or 5, paragraph (a), clause (1), unless the Council on Social Work Education or a similar accreditation body designated by the board has determined through the council's international equivalency determination service that the degree earned is equivalent to the degree required.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.8a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8a.<span class="headnote">Void application.</span></h2>
          <p>An application that is not completed and signed, or that is not accompanied by the correct fee, is void and the board must return it to the applicant, along with any fee submitted.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.8b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8b.<span class="headnote">Investigation.</span></h2>
          <p>By applying for licensure, an applicant authorizes the board to investigate and request additional information to verify completion of all license qualifications.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.8c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8c.<span class="headnote">Application time limit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Within one year of the date that the board receives an application for licensure under this section, the applicant must meet all the requirements for licensure and provide all of the information that the board has requested, or the application must be closed.</p>
          <p>(b) If an applicant graduated from a program in candidacy status with the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accrediting body that the board designates, the applicant must meet all of the requirements for licensure and provide all of the information requested within one year of the date that the board receives an application for licensure under this section or within 30 days of the date the program is approved or denied accreditation, whichever is later, or the application must be closed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Effective date.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An initial license is effective on the day that the board receives the applicable license fee from an applicant approved for licensure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Expiration date.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An initial license expires on the last day of the licensee's birth month in the second calendar year following the effective date of the initial license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.055.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Change in license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A licensee who changes from a licensed social worker to a licensed graduate social worker, or from a licensed graduate social worker to a licensed independent social worker, or from a licensed graduate social worker or licensed independent social worker to a licensed independent clinical social worker, must pay the prorated share of the fee for the new license.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The new license is effective on the day that the board receives the applicable license fee from an applicant approved for the new license.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The new license expires on the same expiration date as the license previously held by the licensee prior to the change in the license.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.055.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.73.0">2007 c 123 s 73,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.8.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 8,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.1.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.15.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 15</a>-30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.23.7.0">2024 c 127 art 23 s 7</a>-10,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.0551">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.0551 PROVISIONAL LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements for a provisional license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An applicant may be issued a provisional license if the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has met the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055#stat.148E.055.2">148E.055, subdivision 2</a>, clauses (1), (3), (4), (5), and (6); subdivision 3, clauses (1), (3), (4), (5), and (6); subdivision 4, clauses (1), (2), (4), (5), (6), and (7); or subdivision 5, paragraph (a), clauses (1), (2), (3), (5), (6), (7), and (8); and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) complies with the requirements of subdivisions 2 to 7.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">License term.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A provisional license is valid until expiration, or until the board issues or denies a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>, or until the board revokes the provisional license, whichever occurs first.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A provisional license expires three years after the effective date of the license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Scope of practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee who is issued a provisional license must comply with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050">148E.050</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee who is issued a provisional license must pay the appropriate license fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Supervised practice requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee who is issued a provisional license must document supervised practice as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.116">148E.116</a>. If a licensee issued a provisional license is granted a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>, the licensee must also meet the supervised practice requirements in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>. The supervised practice completed under a provisional license does not apply to this requirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Evaluation by supervisor.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) After being issued a provisional license under subdivision 1, the licensee's supervisor must submit an evaluation every six months during the first 2,000 hours of social work practice. The evaluation must meet the requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.126">148E.126</a>. The supervisor must meet the eligibility requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.116">148E.116</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) After completion of 2,000 hours of supervised social work practice, the licensee's supervisor must submit a final evaluation and attest to the applicant's ability to engage in the practice of social work competently and ethically.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Completion of requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Upon completion of the requirements for a provisional license under subdivisions 1 to 6, an applicant shall not practice social work in Minnesota except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>, unless licensed according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Disciplinary or other action.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee who is issued a provisional license is subject to the grounds for disciplinary action under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.190">148E.190</a>. The board may also take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.260">148E.260</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> if:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the licensee's supervisor does not submit an evaluation as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.126">148E.126</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) an evaluation submitted according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.126">148E.126</a> indicates that the licensee cannot practice social work competently and ethically; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the licensee does not comply with the requirements of subdivisions 1 to 7.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0551.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.23.12.0">2024 c 127 art 23 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.0551.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.58.0">2007 c 123 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.2.8.0">2009 c 157 art 2 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.2.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/125/#laws.1.35.0">2013 c 125 art 1 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.23.1.0">2024 c 127 art 23 s 1</a>,2,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.0555"><b>148E.0555</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/50/#laws.1.130.0">2019 c 50 art 1 s 130</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.0556"><b>148E.0556</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/50/#laws.1.130.0">2019 c 50 art 1 s 130</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.0557"><b>148E.0557</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/50/#laws.1.130.0">2019 c 50 art 1 s 130</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.060">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.060 TEMPORARY LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.060.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Students or persons not currently licensed in another jurisdiction.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) To be issued a temporary license to practice social work, an applicant who is a student as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a> or a person not licensed or credentialed to practice social work in any jurisdiction must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has submitted a current application for a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has submitted the required fees and documentation for the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has passed the applicable licensure examination required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed all requirements for a baccalaureate or graduate degree in social work from a program accredited by the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accrediting body that the board designates, or a doctorate in social work from an accredited university, including all coursework and internships required to obtain the degree;</p>
          <p>(5) has submitted a completed, signed temporary license application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(6) has paid the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A temporary license is effective on the day that the board receives the application and fee or on the date that all requirements are complete as specified in this subdivision, whichever date is later.</p>
          <p>(c) A temporary license issued under this subdivision expires on the last day of the month six months from the effective date or when a license is issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>, whichever date is earlier. A temporary license is nonrenewable.</p>
          <p>(d) In all professional use of the social worker's name, an individual with a baccalaureate or graduate temporary license must represent themselves as a Temporary License Baccalaureate Social Worker or Temporary License Graduate Social Worker. An individual with a baccalaureate or graduate temporary license must not use the credentials LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW until the individual is issued a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) An individual issued a baccalaureate temporary license under this subdivision is authorized to practice social work as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050#stat.148E.050.2">148E.050, subdivision 2</a>. An individual issued a graduate temporary license under this subdivision is authorized to practice social work as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050#stat.148E.050.3">148E.050, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) An individual issued a temporary license under this subdivision must obtain supervision in compliance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>. Supervised practice obtained with a temporary license applies to the supervised practice requirements for a license issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(g) An individual issued a temporary license under this subdivision may complete continuing education in compliance with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>. Continuing education obtained with a temporary license may be applied to the continuing education requirements of a license issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.060.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote"> Persons currently licensed in another jurisdiction.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) To be issued a temporary license to practice social work, an applicant who is licensed or credentialed to practice social work in another jurisdiction must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) holds an active license or credential to practice social work in another jurisdiction;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has completed the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a> and submitted the required fees;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has submitted a completed, signed temporary license application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) has paid the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) has not engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that was or would be in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A temporary license is effective on the day that the board receives the application and fee or on the date that all requirements are complete as specified in this subdivision, whichever date is later.</p>
          <p>(c) A temporary license issued under this subdivision expires on the last day of the month six months from the effective date or when a license is issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>, whichever date is earlier. A temporary license is nonrenewable.</p>
          <p>(d) The board may not issue a temporary license to an individual more than one time in a 12-month period under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(e) In all professional use of the social worker's name, an individual with a baccalaureate or graduate temporary license must represent themselves as a Temporary License Baccalaureate Social Worker or Temporary License Graduate Social Worker. An individual with a baccalaureate or graduate temporary license must not use the credentials LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW until the individual is issued a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) An individual issued a baccalaureate temporary license under this subdivision is authorized to practice social work as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050#stat.148E.050.2">148E.050, subdivision 2</a>. An individual issued a graduate temporary license under this subdivision is authorized to practice social work as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050#stat.148E.050.3">148E.050, subdivision 3</a>, 4, or 5, consistent with the individual's license or credential in the other jurisdiction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.060.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Applicants graduating from programs in candidacy status.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) To be issued a temporary license to practice social work, an applicant who is a student as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a> or a graduate from a program in candidacy status with the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accrediting body that the board designates, must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) has a current application for a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) has submitted the required fees and documentation for the criminal background check according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) has passed the applicable licensure examination required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed all requirements for a baccalaureate, graduate, or doctoral degree in social work from a program in candidacy status with the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accrediting body that the board designates, including all coursework and internships required to obtain the degree;</p>
          <p>(5) has submitted a completed, signed temporary license application form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(6) has paid the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p>(7) has not engaged in conduct that is in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>. If the applicant has engaged in conduct that is in violation of the standards of practice, the board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A temporary license is effective on the day that the board receives the application and fee or on the date that all requirements are complete as specified in this subdivision, whichever date is later.</p>
          <p>(c) A temporary license issued under this subdivision expires on the last day of the month 12 months from the license's effective date or when a license is issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>, whichever date is earlier. A temporary license is nonrenewable.</p>
          <p>(d) A temporary license issued under this subdivision may be extended upon a showing that the social work program remains in candidacy status with the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accrediting body that the board designates.</p>
          <p>(e) In all professional use of the social worker's name, an individual with a baccalaureate or graduate temporary license must represent themselves as a Temporary License Baccalaureate Social Worker or Temporary License Graduate Social Worker. An individual with a baccalaureate or graduate temporary license must not use the credentials LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW until the individual has a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) An individual who has a baccalaureate temporary license under this subdivision is authorized to practice social work as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050#stat.148E.050.2">148E.050, subdivision 2</a>. An individual who has a graduate temporary license under this subdivision is authorized to practice social work as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050#stat.148E.050.3">148E.050, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(g) An individual issued a temporary license under this subdivision must obtain supervision in compliance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>. Supervised practice obtained with a temporary license applies to the supervised practice requirements for a license issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(h) An individual who has a temporary license under this subdivision may complete continuing education in compliance with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>.
