An individual who earns continuing education hours with a temporary license may apply those continuing education hours to the continuing education requirements of a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(i) If the Council on Social Work Education, the Canadian Association of Schools of Social Work, or a similar accrediting body that the board designates does not grant accreditation to the program in candidacy status, the board must immediately revoke the temporary license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.060.3">

      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.060.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.74.0">2007 c 123 s 74,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.5.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 5</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.31.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 31</a>-33</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.065">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.065 EXEMPTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Other professionals.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Nothing in this chapter may be construed to prevent members of other professions or occupations from performing functions for which they are qualified or licensed. This exception includes but is not limited to: licensed physicians, registered nurses, licensed practical nurses, licensed psychologists, psychological practitioners, probation officers, members of the clergy and Christian Science practitioners, attorneys, marriage and family therapists, alcohol and drug counselors, professional counselors, school counselors, and licensed occupational therapists or licensed occupational therapist assistants. These persons must not, however, hold themselves out to the public by any title or description stating or implying that they are engaged in the practice of social work, or that they are licensed to engage in the practice of social work. Persons engaged in the practice of social work are not exempt from the board's jurisdiction solely by the use of one of the titles in this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Students.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An internship, externship, or any other social work experience that is required for the completion of an accredited program of social work does not constitute the practice of social work under this chapter. Students exempted under this section may use the title "social work intern."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.19.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2014 [Expired, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.11.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 11</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">City, county, and state social workers.</span></h2>
          <p> Beginning July 1, 2016, the licensure of city, county, and state agency social workers is voluntary, except an individual who is newly employed by a city or state agency after July 1, 2016, must be licensed if the individual who provides social work services, as those services are defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010#stat.148E.010.11">148E.010, subdivision 11</a>, paragraph (b), is presented to the public by any title incorporating the words "social work" or "social worker."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2014 [Expired, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.13.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.065.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Tribes and private nonprofit agencies; voluntary licensure.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The licensure of social workers who are employed by federally recognized tribes is voluntary.</p>
          <p>(b) The licensure of private, nonprofit, nontribal agency social workers whose primary service focus addresses ethnic minority populations, and who are themselves members of ethnic minority populations within those agencies, is voluntary until July 1, 2016, when newly employed individuals who practice social work must be licensed as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055#stat.148E.055.1">148E.055, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.065.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.75.0">2007 c 123 s 75,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.10.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 10</a>-14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/108/#laws.2.3.0">2013 c 108 art 2 s 3</a>,44; art 15 s 3,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.6.2.0">2015 c 78 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.38.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 38</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.070">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.070 LICENSE RENEWALS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.070.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License renewal term.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If a license is renewed, the license must be renewed for a two-year renewal term. The renewal term is the period from the effective date of an initial or renewed license to the expiration date of the license.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The effective date of a renewed license is the day following the expiration date of the expired license.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The expiration date of a renewed license is the last day of the licensee's birth month in the second calendar year following the effective date of the renewed license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.070.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote"> License renewal notices.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board must send a notice for license renewal to a licensee at least 45 days before the expiration date of the license. The board may send the renewal notice to the licensee's last known mailing address or electronically. Failure to receive the renewal notice does not relieve a licensee of the obligation to renew a license and to pay the renewal fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.070.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Submitting license renewal applications.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) In order to renew a license, a licensee must submit:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a completed, signed application for license renewal; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the applicable renewal fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
          <p class="bl">The board must receive the completed, signed application and renewal fee prior to midnight on the day of the license expiration date. For electronic renewals, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An application that the applicant has not completed and signed, or that is not accompanied by the correct fee, is void. The board must return the incomplete application and any fee to the applicant.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The completed, signed application must include documentation that the licensee has met the continuing education requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a> and, if applicable, the supervised practice requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) By submitting a renewal application, an applicant authorizes the board to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) investigate any information provided or requested in the application. The board may request that the applicant provide additional information, verification, or documentation;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) conduct an audit to determine if the applicant has met the continuing education requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) if applicable, conduct an audit to determine whether the applicant has met the supervision requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.070.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Renewal late fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An application that is received after the license expiration date must be accompanied by the renewal late fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a> in addition to the applicable renewal fee. The application, renewal fee, and renewal late fee must be received by the board within 60 days of the license expiration date, or the license automatically expires.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.070.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Expired license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A licensee whose license has expired may restore a license to active status by meeting the requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a> or may be relicensed by meeting the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> based on a licensee's conduct before the expiration of the license.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An expired license may be reactivated within one year of the expiration date specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a>. After one year of the expiration date, an individual may apply for a new license according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.070.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.76.0">2007 c 123 s 76,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.34.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 34</a>-36</p>
      </div>

          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.075.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.075.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.075.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2014 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.10.34.0">2015 c 71 art 10 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.075.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>

        </div>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2014 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.10.34.0">2015 c 71 art 10 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.075.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2014 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.10.34.0">2015 c 71 art 10 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.075.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.0751">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.0751 TEMPORARY LEAVE LICENSE STATUS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0751.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Temporary leave.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At the time of license renewal, a licensee may apply for temporary leave license status by:</p>
          <p>(1) submitting a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(2) paying the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) demonstrating to the satisfaction of the board that the licensee is not engaged in the practice of social work as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010, subdivisions 6</a> and 11, in any setting, including settings in which social workers are exempt from licensure according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) By submitting an application for temporary leave license status, an applicant authorizes the board to:</p>
          <p>(1) investigate any information provided or requested in the application. The board may request that the applicant provide additional information, verification, or documentation;</p>
          <p>(2) determine if the applicant has met the continuing education requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) determine whether the applicant has met the supervision requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) An application that the applicant has not completed and signed, or that is not accompanied by the correct fee, is void. The board must return the application to the applicant, along with any fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0751.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Four-year time limit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A temporary leave license status may not exceed four consecutive years. If a licensee fails to restore the license to active status according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a>, the license automatically expires at the end of the four-year term.</p>
          <p>(b) The board must send a notice to a licensee at least 45 days before the expiration date of the temporary leave license status. The board may send the renewal notice to the licensee's last known mailing address or electronically.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0751.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Return to active license status.</span></h2>
          <p>A licensee with temporary leave license status may restore their license to active license status according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a>, including documenting required continuing education hours.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0751.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Prohibition on practice and representation.</span></h2>
          <p>A licensee who has temporary leave license status: (1) must not practice, attempt to practice, offer to practice, or advertise or hold out as being authorized to practice social work; and (2) must include "temporary leave" in any use of the license credential LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0751.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Disciplinary or other action.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may resolve any pending complaints against a licensee before approving an application for temporary leave license status specified in this section. The board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.290">148E.290 </a>against a licensee who has temporary leave license status specified in this section based on conduct occurring before the temporary leave license status or conduct occurring while the license is in temporary leave license status.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.0751.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.37.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 37</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.0752">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.0752 EMERITUS INACTIVE LICENSE STATUS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0752.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Emeritus inactive license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At the time of license renewal, a licensee may apply for emeritus inactive license status by:</p>
          <p>(1) submitting a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that board has specified;</p>
          <p>(2) paying the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) demonstrating to the satisfaction of the board that the licensee is retired from social work practice as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010, subdivisions 6</a> and 11, and will not engage in the practice of social work in any setting, including settings in which social workers are exempt from licensure according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) By submitting an application for emeritus inactive license status, an applicant authorizes the board to:</p>
          <p>(1) investigate any information provided or requested in the application. The board may request that the applicant provide additional information, verification, or documentation;</p>
          <p>(2) determine if the applicant has met the continuing education requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) determine whether the applicant has met the supervision requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) An application that the applicant has not completed and signed, or that is not accompanied by the correct fee, is void. The board must return the application to the applicant, along with any fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0752.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Return to active license status.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A licensee with emeritus inactive license status may restore their license status to active license status during the first four years of the emeritus inactive status according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a>, including documenting required continuing education hours.</p>
          <p>(b) A licensee in emeritus inactive status is not eligible to restore their license to active status after four years. The licensee may reapply for a license according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0752.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Prohibition on practice and representation.</span></h2>
          <p>A licensee who has emeritus inactive license status: (1) must not practice, attempt to practice, offer to practice, or advertise or hold out as being authorized to practice social work; and (2) must include "emeritus inactive" in any use of the license credential LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0752.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Disciplinary or other action.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may resolve any pending complaints against a licensee before approving an application for emeritus inactive license status specified in this section. The board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.290">148E.290</a> against a licensee who has emeritus inactive license status specified in this section based on conduct occurring before the emeritus inactive license status or conduct occurring while the license is in emeritus inactive status.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.0752.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.38.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 38</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.0753">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.0753 EMERITUS ACTIVE LICENSE STATUS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0753.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Emeritus active license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At the time of license renewal, a licensee may apply for emeritus active license status by:</p>
          <p>(1) submitting a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified;</p>
          <p>(2) paying the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) demonstrating to the satisfaction of the board that the licensee is retired from social work practice as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010, subdivisions 6</a> and 11, other than the limited practice allowed in subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(b) By submitting an application for emeritus active license status, an applicant authorizes the board to:</p>
          <p>(1) investigate any information provided or requested in the application. The board may request that the applicant provide additional information, verification, or documentation;</p>
          <p>(2) determine if the applicant has met the continuing education requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) determine whether the applicant has met the supervision requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) An application that the applicant has not completed and signed, or that is not accompanied by the correct fee, is void. The board must return the application to the applicant, along with any fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0753.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Limitation on practice and representation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A licensee who has emeritus active license status is only authorized to engage in:</p>
          <p>(1) pro bono or unpaid social work practice as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010, subdivisions </a>6 and 11; or</p>
          <p>(2) paid social work practice not to exceed 500 clock hours per calendar year for the exclusive purpose of:</p>
          <p>(i) providing licensing supervision as specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>;</p>
          <p>(ii) providing consultation to licensees as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.200#stat.148E.200.2">148E.200, subdivision 2</a>; or</p>
          <p>(iii) presenting continuing education activities as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>, subdivision 9.</p>
          <p>(b) In all professional use of the social worker's name, a licensee must include "emeritus active" in all use of their LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW license credential.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0753.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An applicant who has emeritus active license status may renew their license according to the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.070">148E.070</a> and must pay one-half of the renewal fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180#stat.148E.180.3">148E.180, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Failure to renew an emeritus active license status will result in an expired license as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.070">148E.070</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) At the time of license renewal, a licensee must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the licensee has, during the renewal term, completed one-half of the clock hours of continuing education as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>, including at least two clock hours in social work ethics.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0753.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Return to active license status.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant may restore a license in emeritus active status to active license status according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.0753.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Disciplinary or other action.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may resolve any pending complaints against a licensee before approving an application for emeritus active license status specified in this section. The board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.290">148E.290 </a>against a licensee who has emeritus active license status specified in this section based on conduct occurring before the emeritus active license status or conduct occurring while the license is in emeritus active license status.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.0753.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.39.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 39</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.080">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.080 REACTIVATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.40.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 40</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Reactivation from temporary leave license status.</span></h2>
          <p>To restore a license from temporary leave license status to active status, a licensee must do the following within the time period specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0751#stat.148E.0751.2">148E.0751, subdivision 2</a>:</p>
          <p>(1) submit a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified. An application that the applicant has not completed and signed is void. The board must return the application and any fee to the applicant;</p>
          <p>(2) document compliance with the continuing education requirements specified in subdivision 4; and</p>
          <p>(3) pay the reactivation fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reactivation from emeritus inactive license status.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">To restore a license from emeritus inactive license status to active status, a licensee must do the following within the time period specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0752#stat.148E.0752.2">148E.0752, subdivision 2</a>:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) submit a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified. An application that the applicant has not completed and signed is void. The board must return the application and any fee to the applicant;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) document compliance with the continuing education requirements specified in subdivision 4; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) pay the reactivation fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Reactivation of an expired license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">To restore an expired license to active status, a licensee must do the following within one year of the expiration date:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) submit a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified. An application that the applicant has not completed and signed is void. The board must return the application and any fee to the applicant;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) document compliance with the continuing education requirements that were in effect at the time that the license expired; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) pay the reactivation fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Continuing education requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) At the time of reactivation, a licensee who is on temporary leave license status or who is on emeritus inactive license status must document compliance with the following continuing education requirements:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) obtain at least two continuing education hours in social work ethics; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) complete the board's jurisprudence exam.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A licensee applying for reactivation according to this section may apply for an extension to the continuing education requirements according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>, subdivision 10.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Reactivation of a voluntarily terminated license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">To restore a voluntarily terminated license to active status, a licensee must do the following within one year of the date that the voluntary termination takes effect:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) submit a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified. An application that the applicant has not completed and signed is void. The board must return the application and any fee to the applicant;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) document compliance with the continuing education requirements that were in effect at the time that the license was voluntarily terminated; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) pay the reactivation fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.080.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Reactivation from emeritus active status.</span></h2>
          <p>To restore a license from emeritus active license status to active status, a licensee must do the following while the license remains in emeritus active license status:</p>
          <p>(1) submit a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified. An application that is not completed and signed is void. The board must return the application and any fee to the applicant; and</p>
          <p>(2) pay the reactivation fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.080.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.78.0">2007 c 123 s 78,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.10.16.0">2015 c 71 art 10 s 16</a>,17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.40.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 40</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.085">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.085 VOLUNTARY TERMINATION LICENSE STATUS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.085.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote"> Voluntary termination.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A licensee may apply for voluntary termination of a license if the licensee:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) submits a completed, signed application on a form that the board has provided. For electronic applications, a "signed application" means providing an attestation that the board has specified. An application that the applicant has not completed and signed is void. The board must return the application to the applicant. No fee is required to voluntarily terminate a license; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) demonstrates to the satisfaction of the board that the licensee is not engaged in the practice of social work as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010, subdivisions 6</a> and 11, in any setting except when the individual is exempt from licensure according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) By submitting an application for voluntary termination, an applicant authorizes the board to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) investigate any information provided or requested in the application. The board may request that the applicant provide additional information, verification, or documentation;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) determine if the applicant has met the continuing education requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) determine whether the applicant has met the supervision requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.085.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Return to active license status.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee who has voluntarily terminated a license: (1) may restore the license according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.080">148E.080</a>; or (2) may reapply for a license according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.085.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Prohibition on practice and representation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee who has voluntarily terminated a license: (1) must not use the credentials LSW, LGSW, LISW, or LICSW; and (2) must not practice, attempt to practice, offer to practice, or advertise or hold out as authorized to practice social work, except when the individual is exempt from licensure according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.085.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Disciplinary or other action.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board may resolve any pending complaints against a licensee before approving a request for voluntary termination. The board may take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.290">148E.290</a> against a licensee who voluntarily terminated their license based on conduct occurring before the license was voluntarily terminated.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.085.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.79.0">2007 c 123 s 79,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.41.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 41</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.090">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.090 NAME; CHANGE OF NAME OR ADDRESS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.090.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Name.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensee must use the licensee's legal name or a professional name. If the licensee uses a professional name, the licensee must inform the board in writing of both the licensee's professional name and legal name and must comply with the requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.090.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Legal name change.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Within 30 days after changing the licensee's legal name, a licensee must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) request a new license wall certificate;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) provide legal verification of the name change; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) pay the license wall certificate fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.090.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Professional name change.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Within 30 days after changing the licensee's professional name, a licensee must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) request a new license wall certificate;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) provide a notarized statement attesting to the name change; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) pay the license wall certificate fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.090.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Address or telephone change.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">When a licensee changes a mailing address, home address, work address, email address, or daytime public telephone number, the licensee must notify the board of the change electronically or in writing no more than 30 days after the date of the change.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.090.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.80.0">2007 c 123 s 80,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.095">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.095 LICENSE CERTIFICATE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.095.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License wall certificate.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The board must issue a new license wall certificate when the board issues a new license. No fee in addition to the applicable license fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a> is required.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) To request a duplicate license wall certificate, the licensee must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) submit a request for another license wall certificate in writing; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) submit the license wall certificate fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.095.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.095.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.81.0">2007 c 123 s 81,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.42.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 42</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.100">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.100 LICENSED SOCIAL WORKERS; SUPERVISED PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision required after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After receiving a license from the board as a licensed social worker, the licensed social worker must obtain at least 100 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Practice requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be obtained during the first 4,000 hours of postbaccalaureate social work practice authorized by law. At least four hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Supervised practice obtained prior to August 1, 2011.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding the requirements in subdivisions 1 and 2, the board shall approve hours of supervised practice completed prior to August 1, 2011, which comply with Minnesota Statutes 2010, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.100">148D.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.125">148D.125</a>. These hours must apply to supervised practice requirements in effect as specified in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) Any additional hours of supervised practice obtained effective August 1, 2011, must comply with the increased requirements specified in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Types of supervision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Of the 100 hours of supervision required under subdivision 1:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) 50 hours must be provided through one-on-one supervision. The supervision must be provided either in person or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The board must allow a licensed social worker to satisfy the supervision requirement of this clause with all required hours of supervision provided via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) 50 hours must be provided through: (i) one-on-one supervision, or (ii) group supervision. The supervision may be in person, by telephone, or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The supervision must not be provided by email. Group supervision is limited to six supervisees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Supervisor requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be provided by a supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>. The supervision must be provided by a:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) licensed social worker who has completed the supervised practice requirements;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) licensed graduate social worker, licensed independent social worker, or licensed independent clinical social worker; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120#stat.148E.120.2">148E.120, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Supervisee requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervisee must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to the satisfaction of the supervisor, practice competently and ethically according to professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) receive supervision in the following content areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) development of professional values and responsibilities;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) practice skills;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p class="in">(v) ethical standards of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) submit a supervision plan according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.1">148E.125, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) submit verification of supervised practice according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>, when a licensed social worker applies for the renewal of a license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">After completion of supervision requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensed social worker who fulfills the supervision requirements specified in this section is not required to be supervised after completion of the supervision requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.100.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The social worker and the social worker's supervisor must submit verification that the supervisee has met or has made progress on meeting the applicable supervision requirements according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.100.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.82.0">2007 c 123 s 82,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.9.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 9-14,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.5.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.3.3.0">2022 c 98 art 3 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.105">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.105 LICENSED GRADUATE SOCIAL WORKERS WHO DO NOT PRACTICE CLINICAL SOCIAL WORK; SUPERVISED PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision required after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After receiving a license from the board as a licensed graduate social worker, a licensed graduate social worker not engaged in clinical practice must obtain at least 100 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Practice requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be obtained during the first 4,000 hours of postgraduate social work practice authorized by law. At least four hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Supervised practice obtained prior to August 1, 2011.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding the requirements in subdivisions 1 and 2, the board shall approve hours of supervised practice completed prior to August 1, 2011, which comply with Minnesota Statutes 2010, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.100">148D.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.125">148D.125</a>. These hours shall apply to supervised practice requirements in effect as specified in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) Any additional hours of supervised practice obtained effective August 1, 2011, must comply with the increased requirements specified in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Types of supervision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Of the 100 hours of supervision required under subdivision 1:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) 50 hours must be provided through one-on-one supervision. The supervision must be provided either in person or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The board must allow a licensed graduate social worker to satisfy the supervision requirement of this clause with all required hours of supervision provided via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) 50 hours must be provided through: (i) one-on-one supervision, or (ii) group supervision. The supervision may be in person, by telephone, or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The supervision must not be provided by email. Group supervision is limited to six supervisees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Supervisor requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be provided by a supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>. The supervision must be provided by a:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) licensed independent social worker;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) licensed graduate social worker who has completed the supervised practice requirements;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) licensed independent clinical social worker; or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) a supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120#stat.148E.120.2">148E.120, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Supervisee requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervisee must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to the satisfaction of the supervisor, practice competently and ethically according to professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) receive supervision in the following content areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) development of professional values and responsibilities;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) practice skills;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p class="in">(v) ethical standards of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) submit a supervision plan according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.1">148E.125, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) verify supervised practice according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>, when:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) a licensed graduate social worker applies for the renewal of a license; or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) a licensed graduate social worker applies for a licensed independent social worker license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Supervision not required after completion of supervision requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A licensed graduate social worker who fulfills the supervision requirements specified in subdivisions 1 to 5, and who does not practice clinical social work, is not required to be supervised after completion of the supervision requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker and the social worker's supervisor must submit verification that the supervisee has met or has made progress on meeting the applicable supervision requirements according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.105.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Eligibility to apply for licensure as a licensed independent social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Upon completion of 4,000 hours of social work practice, including at least 100 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section, a licensed graduate social worker is eligible to apply for a licensed independent social worker license according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.110">148E.110</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.105.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.83.0">2007 c 123 s 83,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.15.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 15-19,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.6.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.42.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.3.4.0">2022 c 98 art 3 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.106">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.106 LICENSED GRADUATE SOCIAL WORKERS WHO PRACTICE CLINICAL SOCIAL WORK; SUPERVISED PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision required after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After receiving a license from the board as a licensed graduate social worker, a licensed graduate social worker engaged in clinical practice must obtain at least 200 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section:</p>
          <p>(1) a minimum of four hours and a maximum of eight hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice until the licensed graduate social worker is issued a licensed independent clinical social worker license;</p>
          <p>(2) a minimum of 200 hours of supervision must be completed, in addition to all other requirements according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>, to be eligible to apply for the licensed independent clinical social worker license; and</p>
          <p>(3) the supervisee and supervisor are required to adjust the rate of supervision obtained, based on the ratio of four hours of supervision during every 160 hours of practice, to ensure compliance with the requirements in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Practice requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be obtained:</p>
          <p>(1) in no less than 4,000 hours and no more than 8,000 hours of postgraduate, clinical social work practice authorized by law, including at least 1,800 hours of direct clinical client contact; and</p>
          <p>(2) a minimum of four hours and a maximum of eight hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Supervised practice obtained prior to August 1, 2011.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding the requirements in subdivisions 1 and 2, the board shall approve hours of supervised practice completed prior to August 1, 2011, which comply with Minnesota Statutes 2010, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.100">148D.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.125">148D.125</a>. These hours shall apply to supervised practice requirements in effect as specified in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) Any additional hours of supervised practice obtained effective August 1, 2011, must comply with the increased requirements specified in this section.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding the requirements in subdivision 2, clause (1), direct clinical client contact hours are (i) not required prior to August 1, 2011, and (ii) not required of a licensed graduate social worker engaged in clinical practice with a licensed graduate social worker license issue date prior to August 1, 2011.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Types of supervision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Of the 200 hours of supervision required under subdivision 1:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) 100 hours must be provided through one-on-one supervision. The supervision must be provided either in person or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The board must allow a licensed graduate social worker to satisfy the supervision requirement of this clause with all required hours of supervision provided via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) 100 hours must be provided through: (i) one-on-one supervision, or (ii) group supervision. The supervision may be in person, by telephone, or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The supervision must not be provided by email. Group supervision is limited to six supervisees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Supervisor requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be provided by a supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>. The supervision must be provided by a:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) licensed independent clinical social worker; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>, subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Supervisee requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervisee must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to the satisfaction of the supervisor, practice competently and ethically according to professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) receive supervision in the following content areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) development of professional values and responsibilities;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) practice skills;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p class="in">(v) ethical standards of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) submit a supervision plan according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.1">148E.125, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) verify supervised practice according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>, when:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) a licensed graduate social worker applies for the renewal of a license; or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) a licensed graduate social worker applies for a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Never effective, 2007 c 123 s 138; 2009 c 157 art 3 s 48]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Limit on practice of clinical social work.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except as provided in subdivision 8, a licensed graduate social worker must not engage in clinical social work practice under supervision for more than 8,000 hours. In order to practice clinical social work for more than 8,000 hours, a licensed graduate social worker must obtain a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Notwithstanding the requirements of paragraph (a), the board may grant a licensed graduate social worker permission to engage in clinical social work practice for more than 8,000 hours if the licensed graduate social worker petitions the board and demonstrates to the board's satisfaction that for reasons of personal hardship the licensed graduate social worker should be granted an extension to continue practicing clinical social work under supervision for up to an additional 2,000 hours.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Eligibility to apply for licensure as a licensed independent clinical social worker.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Upon completion of not less than 4,000 hours and not more than 8,000 hours of clinical social work practice, including at least 1,800 hours of direct clinical client contact and 200 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section, a licensed graduate social worker is eligible to apply for a licensed independent clinical social worker license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115#stat.148E.115.1">148E.115, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.106.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker and the social worker's supervisor must submit verification that the supervisee has met or has made progress on meeting the applicable supervision requirements according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.106.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.84.0">2007 c 123 s 84,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.20.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 20</a>-27,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.7.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.3.5.0">2022 c 98 art 3 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.110">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.110 LICENSED INDEPENDENT SOCIAL WORKERS; SUPERVISED PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision required before licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Before becoming licensed as a licensed independent social worker, a person must have obtained at least 100 hours of supervision during 4,000 hours of postgraduate social work practice required by law according to the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.105">148E.105</a>. At least four hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Supervised practice obtained prior to August 1, 2011.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 1, the board shall approve supervised practice hours completed prior to August 1, 2011, which comply with Minnesota Statutes 2010, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.100">148D.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.125">148D.125</a>. These hours must apply to supervised practice requirements in effect as specified in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) Any additional hours of supervised practice obtained on or after August 1, 2011, must comply with the increased requirements in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Licensed independent social workers; clinical social work after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After licensure, a licensed independent social worker must not engage in clinical social work practice except under supervision by a licensed independent clinical social worker who meets the requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120#stat.148E.120.1">148E.120, subdivision 1</a>, or an alternate supervisor designated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120#stat.148E.120.2">148E.120, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Limit on practice of clinical social work.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), a licensed independent social worker must not engage in clinical social work practice under supervision for more than 8,000 hours. In order to practice clinical social work for more than 8,000 hours, a licensed independent social worker must obtain a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Notwithstanding the requirements of paragraph (a), the board may grant a licensed independent social worker permission to engage in clinical social work practice for more than 8,000 hours if the licensed independent social worker petitions the board and demonstrates to the board's satisfaction that for reasons of personal hardship the licensed independent social worker should be granted an extension to continue practicing clinical social work under supervision for up to an additional 2,000 hours.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Licensed independent social workers who do not practice clinical social work after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After licensure, a licensed independent social worker is not required to be supervised if the licensed independent social worker does not practice clinical social work.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Supervision; licensed independent social worker engaged in clinical social work practice.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) After receiving a license from the board as a licensed independent social worker, a licensed independent social worker engaged in clinical social work practice must obtain at least 200 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section.</p>
          <p>(b) A minimum of four hours and a maximum of eight hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice until the licensed independent social worker is issued a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
          <p>(c) A minimum of 200 hours of supervision must be completed, in addition to all other requirements according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>, to be eligible to apply for the licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
          <p>(d) The supervisee and supervisor are required to adjust the rate of supervision obtained based on the ratio of four hours of supervision during every 160 hours of practice to ensure compliance with the requirements in subdivision 1a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Practice requirements after licensure as licensed independent social worker; clinical social work practice.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The supervision required by subdivision 5 must be obtained:</p>
          <p>(1) in no less than 4,000 hours and no more than 8,000 hours of postgraduate clinical social work practice authorized by law, including at least 1,800 hours of direct clinical client contact; and</p>
          <p>(2) a minimum of four hours and a maximum of eight hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), clause (1), direct clinical client contact hours are (i) not required prior to August 1, 2011, and (ii) not required of a licensed independent social worker engaged in clinical practice with a licensed independent social worker license issue date prior to August 1, 2011.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Supervision; clinical social work practice after licensure as licensed independent social worker.</span></h2>
          <p>Of the 200 hours of supervision required under subdivision 5:</p>
          <p>(1) 100 hours must be provided through one-on-one supervision. The supervision must be provided either in person or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The board must allow a licensed independent social worker to satisfy the supervision requirement of this clause with all required hours of supervision provided via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact; and</p>
          <p>(2) 100 hours must be provided through: (i) one-on-one supervision; or (ii) group supervision. The supervision may be in person, by telephone, or via eye-to-eye electronic media, while maintaining visual contact. The supervision must not be provided by email. Group supervision is limited to six supervisees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Supervisor requirements; clinical social work practice after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p>The supervision required by subdivision 5 must be provided by a supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>. The supervision must be provided by a:</p>
          <p>(1) licensed independent clinical social worker; or</p>
          <p>(2) supervisor who meets the requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>, subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Supervisee requirements; clinical social work practice after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p>The supervisee must:</p>
          <p>(1) to the satisfaction of the supervisor, practice competently and ethically according to professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p>(2) receive supervision in the following content areas:</p>
          <p>(i) development of professional values and responsibilities;</p>
          <p>(ii) practice skills;</p>
          <p>(iii) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p>(iv) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p>(v) ethical standards of practice;</p>
          <p>(3) submit a supervision plan according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.1">148E.125, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
          <p>(4) verify supervised practice according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>, when:</p>
          <p>(i) a licensed independent social worker applies for the renewal of a license; or</p>
          <p>(ii) a licensed independent social worker applies for a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Limit on practice of clinical social work.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), a licensed independent social worker must not engage in clinical social work practice under supervision for more than 8,000 hours. In order to practice clinical social work for more than 8,000 hours, a licensed independent social worker must obtain a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding the requirements of paragraph (a), the board may grant a licensed independent social worker permission to engage in clinical social work practice for more than 8,000 hours if the licensed independent social worker petitions the board and demonstrates to the board's satisfaction that for reasons of personal hardship the licensed independent social worker should be granted an extension to continue practicing clinical social work under supervision for up to an additional 2,000 hours.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Eligibility for licensure; licensed independent clinical social worker.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon completion of not less than 4,000 hours and not more than 8,000 hours of clinical social work practice, including at least 1,800 hours of direct clinical client contact and 200 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section, a licensed independent social worker is eligible to apply for a licensed independent clinical social worker license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115#stat.148E.115.1">148E.115, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.110.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p>A social worker and the social worker's supervisor must submit verification that the supervisee has met or has made progress on meeting the applicable supervision requirements according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125#stat.148E.125.3">148E.125, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.110.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.85.0">2007 c 123 s 85,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.28.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 28-38,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.8.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.3.6.0">2022 c 98 art 3 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.115">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.115 LICENSED INDEPENDENT CLINICAL SOCIAL WORKERS; SUPERVISED PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.115.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision before licensure; licensed independent clinical social worker.</span></h2>
