          <p class="in">Before becoming licensed as a licensed independent clinical social worker, a person must have obtained at least 200 hours of supervision at the rate of a minimum of four and a maximum of eight hours of supervision for every 160 hours of practice, in not less than 4,000 hours and not more than 8,000 hours of postgraduate clinical practice required by law, including at least 1,800 hours of direct clinical client contact, according to the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.106">148E.106</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.115.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Supervised practice obtained prior to August 1, 2011.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 and 2, applicants and licensees who have completed hours of supervised practice prior to August 1, 2011, which comply with Minnesota Statutes 2010, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.100">148D.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.125">148D.125</a>, may have that supervised practice applied to the licensing requirement.</p>
          <p>(b) Any additional hours of supervised practice obtained on or after August 1, 2011, must comply with the increased requirements in this section.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding subdivision 1, in order to qualify for the licensed independent clinical social work license, direct clinical client contact hours are:</p>
          <p>(1) not required prior to August 1, 2011; and</p>
          <p>(2) not required of either a licensed graduate social worker or a licensed independent social worker engaged in clinical practice with a license issued prior to August 1, 2011.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.115.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">No supervision required after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After licensure, a licensed independent clinical social worker is not required to be supervised.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.115.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.86.0">2007 c 123 s 86,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.39.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 39,40,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.9.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.116">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.116 PROVISIONAL LICENSE; SUPERVISED PRACTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.116.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision required after licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">After receiving a provisional license from the board, the licensee must obtain at least 37.5 hours of supervision according to the requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.116.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Practice requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be obtained during the first 2,000 hours of social work practice after the effective date of the provisional license. At least three hours of supervision must be obtained during every 160 hours of practice under a provisional license until a permanent license is issued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.116.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Types of supervision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Half of the supervision hours required by subdivision 1 must consist of one-on-one supervision. The supervision must be provided either in person or via eye-to-eye electronic media while maintaining visual contact.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Half of the supervision hours must consist of one or more of the following types of supervision:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) one-on-one supervision provided in person or via eye-to-eye electronic media while maintaining visual contact; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) group supervision provided in person, by telephone, or via eye-to-eye electronic media while maintaining visual contact.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Group supervision must not exceed six supervisees.</p>
          <p>(d) Supervision must not be provided by email.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.116.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Supervisor requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The supervision required by subdivision 1 must be provided by a supervisor who meets the requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a> and has completed 30 hours of training in supervision, which may be satisfied by completing academic coursework in supervision or continuing education courses in supervision as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010#stat.148E.010.18">148E.010, subdivision 18</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Supervision must be provided:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) if the supervisee is not engaged in clinical practice and the supervisee has a provisional license to practice as a licensed social worker, by:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) a licensed social worker who has completed the supervised practice requirements;</p>
          <p>(ii) a licensed graduate social worker who has completed the supervised practice requirements;</p>
          <p>(iii) a licensed independent social worker; or</p>
          <p>(iv) a licensed independent clinical social worker;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) if the supervisee is not engaged in clinical practice and the supervisee has a provisional license to practice as a licensed graduate social worker, licensed independent social worker, or licensed independent clinical social worker, by:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) a licensed graduate social worker who has completed the supervised practice requirements;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) a licensed independent social worker; or</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) a licensed independent clinical social worker;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) if the supervisee is engaged in clinical practice and the supervisee has a provisional license to practice as a licensed graduate social worker, licensed independent social worker, or licensed independent clinical social worker, by a licensed independent clinical social worker; or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) by a supervisor who meets the requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120#stat.148E.120.2">148E.120, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.116.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.2.14.0">2009 c 157 art 2 s 14</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.116.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.59.0">2007 c 123 s 59</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.2.10.0">2009 c 157 art 2 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.3.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.23.3.0">2024 c 127 art 23 s 3</a>,4,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.120">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.120 REQUIREMENTS OF SUPERVISORS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.120.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervisors licensed as social workers.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except as provided in subdivision 2, to be eligible to provide supervision under this section, a social worker must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) have completed 30 hours of training in supervision through coursework from an accredited college or university, or through continuing education in compliance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) be competent in the activities being supervised; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) attest, on a form provided by the board, that the social worker has met the applicable requirements specified in this section and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>. The board may audit the information provided to determine compliance with the requirements of this section.</p>
          <p>(b) A licensed independent clinical social worker providing clinical licensing supervision to a licensed graduate social worker or a licensed independent social worker must have at least 2,000 hours of experience in authorized social work practice, including 1,000 hours of experience in clinical practice after obtaining a licensed independent clinical social worker license.</p>
          <p>(c) A licensed social worker, licensed graduate social worker, licensed independent social worker, or licensed independent clinical social worker providing nonclinical licensing supervision must have completed the supervised practice requirements specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.105">148E.105</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.106">148E.106</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.110">148E.110</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>, as applicable.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.120.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Alternate supervisors.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The board may approve an alternate supervisor as determined in this subdivision. The board shall approve up to 25 percent of the required supervision hours by a mental health professional who is competent and qualified to provide supervision according to the mental health professional's respective licensing board, as established by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245I.04#stat.245I.04.2">245I.04, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The board shall approve up to 100 percent of the required supervision hours by an alternate supervisor if the board determines that:</p>
          <p>(1) there are five or fewer supervisors in the county where the licensee practices social work who meet the applicable licensure requirements in subdivision 1;</p>
          <p>(2) the supervisor is an unlicensed social worker who is employed in, and provides the supervision in, a setting exempt from licensure by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>, and who has qualifications equivalent to the applicable requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) the supervisor is a social worker engaged in authorized social work practice in Iowa, Manitoba, North Dakota, Ontario, South Dakota, or Wisconsin, and has the qualifications equivalent to the applicable requirements in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>; or</p>
          <p>(4) the applicant or licensee is engaged in nonclinical authorized social work practice outside of Minnesota and the supervisor meets the qualifications equivalent to the applicable requirements in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>, or the supervisor is an equivalent mental health professional, as determined by the board, who is credentialed by a state, territorial, provincial, or foreign licensing agency; or</p>
          <p>(5) the applicant or licensee is engaged in clinical authorized social work practice outside of Minnesota and the supervisor meets qualifications equivalent to the applicable requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>, or the supervisor is an equivalent mental health professional as determined by the board, who is credentialed by a state, territorial, provincial, or foreign licensing agency.</p>
          <p>(c) In order for the board to consider an alternate supervisor under this section, the licensee must:</p>
          <p>(1) request in the supervision plan and verification submitted according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a> that an alternate supervisor conduct the supervision; and</p>
          <p>(2) describe the proposed supervision and the name and qualifications of the proposed alternate supervisor. The board may audit the information provided to determine compliance with the requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.120.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.87.0">2007 c 123 s 87,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.41.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 41,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.15.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.17.8.0">2021 c 30 art 17 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.43.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 43</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.125">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.125 DOCUMENTATION OF SUPERVISION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.125.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision plan.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker must submit, on a form provided by the board, a supervision plan for meeting the supervision requirements specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.120">148E.120</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The supervision plan must be submitted no later than 60 days after the licensee begins a social work practice position after becoming licensed.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For failure to submit the supervision plan within 60 days after beginning a social work practice position, a licensee must pay the supervision plan late fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a> when the licensee applies for license renewal.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) A license renewal application submitted according to paragraph (a) must not be approved unless the board has received a supervision plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The supervision plan must include the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the name of the supervisee, the name of the agency in which the supervisee is being supervised, and the supervisee's position title;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the name and qualifications of the person providing the supervision;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the number of hours of one-on-one in-person supervision and the number and type of additional hours of supervision to be completed by the supervisee;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the supervisee's position description;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) a brief description of the supervision the supervisee will receive in the following content areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) clinical practice, if applicable;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) development of professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) practice methods;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) ethical standards of practice; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) if applicable, a detailed description of the supervisee's clinical social work practice, addressing:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the client population, the range of presenting issues, and the diagnoses;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) the clinical modalities that were utilized; and</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) the process utilized for determining clinical diagnoses, including the diagnostic instruments used and the role of the supervisee in the diagnostic process.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The board must receive a revised supervision plan within 60 days of any of the following changes:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the supervisee has a new supervisor;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the supervisee begins a new social work position;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the scope or content of the supervisee's social work practice changes substantially;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the number of practice or supervision hours changes substantially; or</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the type of supervision changes as supervision is described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100#stat.148E.100.3">148E.100, subdivision 3</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.105#stat.148E.105.3">148E.105, subdivision 3</a>, or as required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.115">148E.115</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) For failure to submit a revised supervision plan as required in paragraph (f), a supervisee must pay the supervision plan late fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>, when the supervisee applies for license renewal.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) The board must approve the supervisor and the supervision plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.125.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Never effective, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.88.0">2007 c 123 s 88</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.48.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 48</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.125.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The board must receive verification of supervised practice when:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the licensee submits the license renewal application form; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) an applicant applies for a license as a licensed independent social worker or as a licensed independent clinical social worker.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) When verification of supervised practice is required according to paragraph (a), the board must receive from the supervisor the following information on a form provided by the board:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the name of the supervisee, the name of the agency in which the supervisee is being supervised, and the supervisee's position title;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the name and qualifications of the supervisor;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the number of hours and dates of each type of supervision completed;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the supervisee's position description;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) a declaration that the supervisee has not engaged in conduct in violation of the standards of practice specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) a declaration that the supervisee has practiced ethically and competently according to professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) a list of the content areas in which the supervisee has received supervision, including the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) clinical practice, if applicable;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) development of professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) practice methods;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) ethical standards of practice; and</p>
          <p class="in">(8) if applicable, a detailed description of the supervisee's clinical social work practice, addressing:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the client population, the range of presenting issues, and the diagnoses;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) the clinical modalities that were utilized; and</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) the process utilized for determining clinical diagnoses, including the diagnostic instruments used and the role of the supervisee in the diagnostic process.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The information provided on the verification form must demonstrate to the board's satisfaction that the supervisee has met the applicable supervised practice requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.125.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Alternative verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding the requirements of subdivision 3, the board may accept alternative verification of supervised practice if a supervisee demonstrates to the satisfaction of the board that the supervisee is unable to locate a former supervisor to provide the required information.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.125.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.88.0">2007 c 123 s 88,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.42.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 42,43,49</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.126">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.126 PROVISIONAL LICENSE; DOCUMENTATION OF SUPERVISION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.126.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supervision plan.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An applicant granted a provisional license must submit, on a form provided by the board, a supervision plan for meeting the supervision requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.116">148E.116</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The supervision plan must be submitted no later than 30 days after the licensee begins a social work practice position.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The supervision plan must include the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the name of the supervisee, the name of the agency in which the supervisee is being supervised, and the supervisee's position title;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the name and qualifications of the person providing the supervision;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the number of hours of one-on-one supervision and the number and type of additional hours of supervision to be completed by the supervisee;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the supervisee's position description;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) a brief description of the supervision the supervisee will receive in the following content areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) clinical practice, if applicable;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) development of professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) practice methods;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) ensuring continuing competence; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) ethical standards of practice; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) if applicable, a detailed description of the supervisee's clinical social work practice, addressing:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the client population, the range of presenting issues, and the diagnoses;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) the clinical modalities that were utilized; and</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) the process utilized for determining clinical diagnoses, including the diagnostic instruments used and the role of the supervisee in the diagnostic process.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The board must receive a revised supervision plan within 30 days of any of the following changes:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the supervisee has a new supervisor;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the supervisee begins a new social work position;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the scope or content of the supervisee's social work practice changes substantially;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the number of practice or supervision hours changes substantially; or</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the type of supervision changes as supervision is described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.116">148E.116</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The board must approve the supervisor and the supervision plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.126.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Evaluation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) When a licensee's supervisor submits an evaluation to the board according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.0551#stat.148E.0551.6">148E.0551, subdivision 6</a>, the supervisee and supervisor must provide the following information on a form provided by the board:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the name of the supervisee, the name of the agency in which the supervisee is being supervised, and the supervisee's position title;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the name and qualifications of the supervisor;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the number of hours and dates of each type of supervision completed;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the supervisee's position description;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) a declaration that the supervisee has not engaged in conduct in violation of the standards of practice in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) a declaration that the supervisee has practiced competently and ethically according to professional social work knowledge, skills, and values; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) on a form provided by the board, an evaluation of the licensee's practice in the following areas:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) development of professional social work knowledge, skills, and values;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) practice methods;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) authorized scope of practice;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) ethical standards of practice; and</p>
          <p class="in">(v) clinical practice, if applicable.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The supervisor must attest to the satisfaction of the board that the supervisee has met or has made progress on meeting the applicable supervised practice requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.126.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Alternative verification of supervised practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding the requirements of subdivision 2, the board may accept alternative verification of supervised practice if a supervisee demonstrates that the supervisee is unable to locate a former supervisor to provide the required information.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.126.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.60.0">2007 c 123 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.2.11.0">2009 c 157 art 2 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.4.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.23.5.0">2024 c 127 art 23 s 5</a>,6,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.130">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.130 CONTINUING EDUCATION REQUIREMENTS FOR LICENSEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Total clock hours required.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A licensee must complete 40 hours of continuing education for each two-year renewal term. At the time of license renewal, a licensee must provide evidence satisfactory to the board that the licensee has completed the required continuing education hours during the previous renewal term. Of the total clock hours required:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) all licensees must complete:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) two hours in social work ethics as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) four hours in cultural responsiveness;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) licensed independent clinical social workers must complete 12 clock hours in one or more of the clinical content areas specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055#stat.148E.055.5">148E.055, subdivision 5</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2);</p>
          <p class="in">(3) licensees providing licensing supervision according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>, must complete six clock hours in supervision as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) no more than half of the required clock hours may be completed via continuing education independent learning as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the licensee's renewal term is prorated to be less or more than 24 months, the total number of required clock hours is prorated proportionately.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Increased clock hours required effective August 1, 2011.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding the requirements in subdivision 8, the clock hours specified in subdivision 1 apply to all new licenses issued effective August 1, 2011, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Any licensee issued a license prior to August 1, 2011, under Minnesota Statutes 2010, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148D.055">148D.055, </a>must comply with the increased clock hours in subdivision 1 and must document the clock hours at the first two-year renewal term after August 1, 2011.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">New content clock hours required effective July 1, 2021.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The content clock hours in subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), apply to all new licenses issued effective July 1, 2021, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Any licensee issued a license prior to July 1, 2021, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a> must comply with the clock hours in subdivision 1, including the content clock hours in subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), at the first two-year renewal term after July 1, 2021.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Acceptable content.</span></h2>
          <p>The continuing education must:</p>
          <p>(1) promote the standards of practice described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) contribute to the practice of social work as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Acceptable continuing education activities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A continuing education activity must be one of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) academic coursework at an institution of higher learning. One credit of coursework in a semester-based academic institution is the equivalent of 15 clock hours;</p>
          <p>(2) educational workshops, seminars, conferences, or live webinars;</p>
          <p>(3) staff training from an employer; or</p>
          <p>(4) continuing education independent learning as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A continuing education activity automatically meets the requirements of this section if the licensee completes it through:</p>
          <p>(1) a board-approved continuing education provider;</p>
          <p>(2) a continuing education provider or program approved by the Association of Social Work Boards or a similar examination body that the board has designated; or</p>
          <p>(3) a continuing education program approved by the National Association of Social Workers.</p>
          <p>(c) The board does not require providers and programs to be preapproved, but the continuing education activity must meet the requirements specified in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Extension.</span></h2>
          <p>At the time of renewal, the board may grant a onetime extension to complete the required clock hours when a licensee is unable to comply with this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Records retention.</span></h2>
          <p>For one year following the expiration date of a license, the licensee must maintain documentation of clock hours earned during the previous renewal term. The documentation must include the following:</p>
          <p>(1) for educational workshops, seminars, conferences, or live webinars, documentation of attendance issued by the presenter or sponsor must include the:</p>
          <p>(i) name of the sponsor;</p>
          <p>(ii) title and date of the activity;</p>
          <p>(iii) number of clock hours; and</p>
          <p>(iv) names of the presenters;</p>
          <p>(2) for academic coursework at an institution of higher learning, a transcript must include the:</p>
          <p>(i) name of the institution;</p>
          <p>(ii) title of the course;</p>
          <p>(iii) dates of the course; and</p>
          <p>(iv) number of credits completed or audited;</p>
          <p>(3) for an employer's staff training, an employer's documentation of attendance must include the:</p>
          <p>(i) name of the employer;</p>
          <p>(ii) title of the training;</p>
          <p>(iii) date of the training; and</p>
          <p>(iv) number of clock hours completed; and</p>
          <p>(4) for continuing education independent learning, a written summary of the activity conducted must include the:</p>
          <p>(i) topics covered;</p>
          <p>(ii) applicability of the activity to the licensee's social work practice;</p>
          <p>(iii) titles and authors or presenters of materials reviewed;</p>
          <p>(iv) dates the licensee conducted the activity; and</p>
          <p>(v) number of clock hours completed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.130.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Audits.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board may audit licensees at the time of license renewal or reactivation to determine the licensee's compliance with the requirements of this section.</p>
          <p>(b) When the board audits a licensee, the licensee must provide the documentation specified in subdivision 11.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.130.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.89.0">2007 c 123 s 89,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/157/#laws.3.44.0">2009 c 157 art 3 s 44-46,49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.3.10.0">2012 c 187 art 3 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.43.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 43</a>-48; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.4.13.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 4 s 13</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.55.0">2023 c 25 s 55</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.135"><b>148E.135</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.140"><b>148E.140</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.145">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.145 CONTINUING EDUCATION PROVIDERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Board approval.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For the board to approve a continuing education provider, the provider must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) submit a completed application form that the board has provided;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) agree to comply with the requirements of this section;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) submit a signed statement that indicates that the provider agrees to provide documentation of attendance that meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130, subdivision </a>11, to each participant in the provider's program; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) pay the continuing education provider fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An approval is valid for one year from the date that the board receives the application and fee.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) During the one-year period, an approved provider may provide no more than the number of clock hours for which the provider has applied as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a>, subdivision 4. If the provider wishes to offer additional clock hours during the one-year period, the provider must submit another application and fee to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.49.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 49</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Continuing education activity requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An approved provider must ensure that all continuing education activities:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) promote the standards of practice described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) contribute to the practice of social work as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.010">148E.010</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) are presented by individuals with expertise in the material or content.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An approved provider must provide documentation of completion to each participant that includes the information required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.130">148E.130</a>, subdivision 11.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.49.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 49</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Records retention.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An approved provider must maintain the following information for three years following the end of each program activity:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the program's title;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) a description of the program's content and objectives;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the program's date;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the program's number of clock hours;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) whether the activity was presented as a live interactive activity or continuing education independent learning;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the names and qualifications of the program's presenters; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) a list of the program's attendees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Audits.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board may audit an approved provider to determine compliance with the requirements of this section.</p>
          <p>(b) An approved provider audited by the board must provide the documentation specified in subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.145.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may revoke approval of an approved provider if the board determines the provider failed to meet the requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.145.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.92.0">2007 c 123 s 92,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.49.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 49</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.150"><b>148E.150</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.155"><b>148E.155</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.160"><b>148E.160</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.165"><b>148E.165</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.148E.170"><b>148E.170</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/79/#laws.3.50.0">2020 c 79 art 3 s 50</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.175">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.175 FEES.</h1>
        <p class="in">The fees specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.180">148E.180</a> are nonrefundable and must be deposited in the state government special revenue fund.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.175.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.98.0">2007 c 123 s 98,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.180">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.180 FEE AMOUNTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Nonrefundable application fees for licensure may not exceed the following amounts but may be adjusted lower by board action:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for a licensed social worker, $75;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for a licensed graduate social worker, $75;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for a licensed independent social worker, $75;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for a licensed independent clinical social worker, $75;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for a temporary license, $50; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for a license by endorsement, $115.</p>
          <p class="in">The fee for criminal background checks is the fee charged by the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension. The criminal background check fee must be included with the application fee as required according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">License fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Nonrefundable license fees may not exceed the following amounts but may be adjusted lower by board action:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for a licensed social worker, $115;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for a licensed graduate social worker, $210;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for a licensed independent social worker, $305;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for a licensed independent clinical social worker, $335;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for an emeritus inactive license, $65;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for an emeritus active license, one-half of the renewal fee specified in subdivision 3; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) for a temporary leave fee, the same as the renewal fee specified in subdivision 3.</p>
          <p class="in">If the licensee's initial license term is less or more than 24 months, the required license fees must be prorated proportionately.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Renewal fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Nonrefundable renewal fees for licensure may not exceed the following amounts but may be adjusted lower by board action:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for a licensed social worker, $115;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for a licensed graduate social worker, $210;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for a licensed independent social worker, $305; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for a licensed independent clinical social worker, $335.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Continuing education provider fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Continuing education provider fees are the following nonrefundable amounts:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) for a provider who offers programs totaling one to eight clock hours in a one-year period according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>, $50;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for a provider who offers programs totaling nine to 16 clock hours in a one-year period according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>, $100;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) for a provider who offers programs totaling 17 to 32 clock hours in a one-year period according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>, $200;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) for a provider who offers programs totaling 33 to 48 clock hours in a one-year period according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>, $400; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) for a provider who offers programs totaling 49 or more clock hours in a one-year period according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.145">148E.145</a>, $600.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Late fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Late fees are the following nonrefundable amounts:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) renewal late fee, one-fourth of the renewal fee specified in subdivision 3;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) supervision plan late fee, $40; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) license late fee, $100 plus the prorated share of the license fee specified in subdivision 2 for the number of months during which the individual practiced social work without a license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">License cards and wall certificates.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The nonrefundable fee for a license card as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.095">148E.095</a> is $10.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The nonrefundable fee for a license wall certificate as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.095">148E.095 </a>is $30.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.180.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Reactivation fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Reactivation fees are the following nonrefundable amounts:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) reactivation from a temporary leave or emeritus status, the prorated share of the renewal fee specified in subdivision 3; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) reactivation of an expired license, 1-1/2 times the renewal fees specified in subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.180.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.99.0">2007 c 123 s 99,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.11.5.0">2009 c 79 art 11 s 5</a>-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.10.18.0">2015 c 71 art 10 s 18</a>,19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.10.18.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 10 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.185">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.185 PURPOSE OF COMPLIANCE LAWS.</h1>
        <p class="in">The purpose of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.185">148E.185</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.290">148E.290</a> is to protect the public by ensuring that all persons licensed as social workers meet minimum standards of practice. The board shall promptly and fairly investigate and resolve all complaints alleging violations of statutes and rules that the board is empowered to enforce and (1) take appropriate disciplinary action, adversarial action, or other action justified by the facts, or (2) enter into corrective action agreements or stipulations to cease practice, when doing so is consistent with the board's obligation to protect the public.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.185.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.100.0">2007 c 123 s 100,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.190">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.190 GROUNDS FOR ACTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.190.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The grounds for action in subdivisions 2 to 4 and the standards of practice requirements in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a> apply to all licensees and applicants.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.190.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Violations.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board has grounds to take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> when a social worker violates:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a statute or rule enforced by the board, including this section and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) a federal or state law or rule related to the practice of social work; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) an order, stipulation, or agreement agreed to or issued by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.190.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Conduct before licensure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A violation of the requirements specified in this section and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a> is grounds for the board to take action under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a>. The board's jurisdiction to exercise the powers provided in this section extends to an applicant or licensee's conduct that occurred before licensure if the conduct:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) did not meet the minimum accepted and prevailing standards of professional social work practice at the time the conduct occurred; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) adversely affects the applicant or licensee's present ability to practice social work in conformity with the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.195">148E.195</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.240">148E.240</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.190.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Unauthorized practice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board has grounds to take action according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> when a social worker:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) practices outside the scope of practice authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.050">148E.050</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) engages in the practice of social work without a social work license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.060">148E.060</a>, except when the social worker is exempt from licensure under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) provides social work services to a client who receives social work services in this state, and is not licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.060">148E.060</a>, except when the social worker is exempt from licensure under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.065">148E.065</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.190.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.101.0">2007 c 123 s 101,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.195">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.195 REPRESENTATIONS TO CLIENTS AND PUBLIC.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.195.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Required displays and information for clients.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker must conspicuously display at the social worker's places of practice, or make available as a handout for all clients, information that the client has the right to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) be informed of the social worker's license status, education, training, and experience;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) examine public data on the social worker maintained by the board;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) report a complaint about the social worker's practice to the board; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) be informed of the board's mailing address, email address, website address, and telephone number.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A social worker must conspicuously display the social worker's wall certificate at the social worker's places of practice and office locations. Additional wall certificates may be requested according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.095">148E.095</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.195.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2014 [Expired, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.16.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.195.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Representations.</span></h2>
          <p>Effective July 1, 2016:</p>
          <p>(a) No applicant or other individual may be represented to the public by any title incorporating the words "social work" or "social worker" unless the individual is employed by a county or holds a license according to this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) In all professional use of a social worker's name, the social worker must use the license designation "LSW" or "licensed social worker" for a licensed social worker, "LGSW" or "licensed graduate social worker" for a licensed graduate social worker, "LISW" or "licensed independent social worker" for a licensed independent social worker, or "LICSW" or "licensed independent clinical social worker" for a licensed independent clinical social worker.</p>
          <p>(c) Public statements or advertisements must not be untruthful, misleading, false, fraudulent, deceptive, or potentially exploitative of clients, former clients, interns, students, supervisees, or the public.</p>
          <p>(d) A social worker must not:</p>
          <p>(1) use licensure status as a claim, promise, or guarantee of successful service;</p>
          <p>(2) obtain a license by cheating or employing fraud or deception;</p>
          <p>(3) make false statements or misrepresentations to the board or in materials submitted to the board; or</p>
          <p>(4) engage in conduct that has the potential to deceive or defraud a social work client, intern, student, supervisee, or the public.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.195.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Information on credentials.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker must provide accurate and factual information concerning the social worker's credentials, education, training, and experience when the information is requested by clients, potential clients, or other persons or organizations.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A social worker must not misrepresent directly or by implication the social worker's license, degree, professional certifications, affiliations, or other professional qualifications in any oral or written communications to clients, potential clients, or other persons or organizations. A social worker must take reasonable steps to prevent such misrepresentations by other social workers.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A social worker must not hold out as a person licensed as a social worker without having a social work license according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.060">148E.060</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) A social worker must not misrepresent directly or by implication (1) affiliations with institutions or organizations, or (2) purposes or characteristics of institutions or organizations with which the social worker is or has been affiliated.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.195.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.102.0">2007 c 123 s 102,138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/197/#laws.1.16.0">2012 c 197 art 1 s 16</a>,17</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.200">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.200 COMPETENCE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.200.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Competence.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker must provide services and hold out as competent only to the extent the social worker's education, training, license, consultation received, supervision experience, or other relevant professional experience demonstrate competence in the services provided. A social worker must make a referral to a competent professional when the services required are beyond the social worker's competence or authorized scope of practice.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) When generally recognized standards do not exist with respect to an emerging area of practice, including but not limited to providing social work services through electronic means, a social worker must take the steps necessary, such as consultation or supervision, to ensure the competence of the social worker's work and to protect clients from harm.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.200.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Supervision or consultation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding the completion of supervision requirements as specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.100">148E.100</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.125">148E.125</a>, a social worker must obtain supervision or engage in consultation when appropriate or necessary for competent and ethical practice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.200.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Delegation of social work responsibilities.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker must not delegate a social work responsibility to another individual when the social worker knows or reasonably should know that the individual is not licensed when required to be licensed according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.055">148E.055</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.060">148E.060</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A social worker must not delegate a social work responsibility to another individual when the social worker knows or reasonably should know that the individual is not competent to assume the responsibility or perform the task.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.200.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.103.0">2007 c 123 s 103,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.205">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.205 IMPAIRMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.205.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Grounds for action.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board has grounds to take action under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> when a social worker is unable to practice with reasonable skill and safety by reason of illness, use of alcohol, drugs, chemicals, or any other materials, or as a result of any mental, physical, or psychological condition.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.205.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Self-reporting.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker regulated by the board who is unable to practice with reasonable skill and safety by reason of illness, use of alcohol, drugs, chemicals, or any other materials, or as a result of any mental, physical, or psychological condition, must report to the board or the health professionals services program.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.205.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.104.0">2007 c 123 s 104,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.210">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.210 PROFESSIONAL AND ETHICAL CONDUCT.</h1>
        <p class="in">The board has grounds to take action under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.255">148E.255</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148E.270">148E.270</a> when a social worker:</p>
        <p class="in">(1) engages in unprofessional or unethical conduct, including any departure from or failure to conform to the minimum accepted ethical and other prevailing standards of professional social work practice, without actual injury to a social work client, intern, student, supervisee, or the public needing to be established;</p>
        <p class="in">(2) engages in conduct that has the potential to cause harm to a client, intern, student, supervisee, or the public;</p>
        <p class="in">(3) demonstrates a willful or careless disregard for the health, welfare, or safety of a client, intern, student, or supervisee; or</p>
        <p class="in">(4) engages in acts or conduct adversely affecting the applicant or licensee's current ability or fitness to engage in social work practice, whether or not the acts or conduct occurred while engaged in the practice of social work.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.210.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.105.0">2007 c 123 s 105,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.215">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.215 RESPONSIBILITIES TO CLIENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.215.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Responsibility to clients.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker's primary professional responsibility is to the client. A social worker must respect the client's interests, including the interest in self-determination, except when required to do otherwise by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.215.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Nondiscrimination.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must not discriminate against a client, intern, student, or supervisee or in providing services to a client, intern, or supervisee on the basis of age, gender, sexual orientation, race, color, national origin, religion, illness, disability, political affiliation, or social or economic status.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.215.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Research.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">When undertaking research activities, a social worker must use accepted protocols for the protection of human subjects, including (1) establishing appropriate safeguards to protect the subject's vulnerability, and (2) obtaining the subjects' informed consent.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.148E.215.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/123/#laws.0.106.0">2007 c 123 s 106,138</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.148E.220">
        <h1 class="shn">148E.220 RELATIONSHIPS WITH CLIENTS, FORMER CLIENTS, AND OTHERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Social worker responsibility.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker is responsible for acting professionally in relationships with clients or former clients. A client or a former client's initiation of, or attempt to engage in, or request to engage in, a personal, sexual, or business relationship is not a defense to a violation of this section.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) When a relationship is permitted by this section, social workers who engage in such a relationship assume the full burden of demonstrating that the relationship will not be detrimental to the client or the professional relationship.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Professional boundaries.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must maintain appropriate professional boundaries with a client. A social worker must not engage in practices with clients that create an unacceptable risk of client harm or of impairing a social worker's objectivity or professional judgment. A social worker must not act or fail to act in a way that, as judged by a reasonable and prudent social worker, inappropriately encourages the client to relate to the social worker outside of the boundaries of the professional relationship, or in a way that interferes with the client's ability to benefit from social work services from the social worker.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Misuse of professional relationship.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must not use the professional relationship with a client, student, supervisee, or intern to further the social worker's personal, emotional, financial, sexual, religious, political, or business benefit or interests.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Improper termination.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must not terminate a professional relationship for the purpose of beginning a personal, sexual, or business relationship with a client.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Personal relationship with client.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), a social worker must not engage in a personal relationship with a client that creates a risk of client harm or of impairing a social worker's objectivity or professional judgment.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), if a social worker is unable to avoid a personal relationship with a client, the social worker must take appropriate precautions, such as consultation or supervision, to address the potential for risk of client harm or of impairing a social worker's objectivity or professional judgment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Personal relationship with former client.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker may engage in a personal relationship with a former client after appropriate termination of the professional relationship, except:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) as prohibited by subdivision 8; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) if a reasonable and prudent social worker would conclude after appropriate assessment that (i) the former client is emotionally dependent on the social worker or continues to relate to the social worker as a client, or (ii) the social worker is emotionally dependent on the client or continues to relate to the former client as a social worker.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Sexual conduct with client.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with a client.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Sexual conduct with former client.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker who has engaged in diagnosing, counseling, or treating a client with mental, emotional, or behavioral disorders must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with the former client under any circumstances for a period of two years following the termination of the professional relationship. After two years following the termination of the professional relationship, a social worker who has engaged in diagnosing, counseling, or treating a client with a mental, emotional, or behavioral disorder must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with the former client under any circumstances unless:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the social worker did not intentionally or unintentionally coerce, exploit, deceive, or manipulate the former client at any time;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the social worker did not represent to the former client that sexual conduct with the social worker is consistent with or part of the client's treatment;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the social worker's sexual conduct was not detrimental to the former client at any time;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the former client is not emotionally dependent on the social worker and does not continue to relate to the social worker as a client; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the social worker is not emotionally dependent on the client and does not continue to relate to the former client as a social worker.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If there is an alleged violation of paragraph (a), the social worker assumes the full burden of demonstrating to the board that the social worker did not intentionally or unintentionally coerce, exploit, deceive, or manipulate the client, and the social worker's sexual conduct was not detrimental to the client at any time. Upon request, a social worker must provide information to the board addressing:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the amount of time that has passed since termination of services;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the duration, intensity, and nature of services;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the circumstances of termination of services;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the former client's emotional, mental, and behavioral history;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the former client's current emotional, mental, and behavioral status;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the likelihood of adverse impact on the former client; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) the existence of actions, conduct, or statements made by the social worker during the course of services suggesting or inviting the possibility of a sexual relationship with the client following termination of services.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A social worker who has provided social work services other than those described in paragraph (a) to a client must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with the former client if a reasonable and prudent social worker would conclude after appropriate assessment that engaging in such behavior with the former client would create an unacceptable risk of harm to the former client.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Sexual conduct with student, supervisee, or intern.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A social worker must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with a student while the social worker has authority over any part of the student's academic program.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A social worker supervising an intern must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with the intern during the course of the internship.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A social worker practicing social work as a supervisor must not engage in or suggest sexual conduct with a supervisee during the period of supervision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Sexual harassment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must not engage in any physical, oral, written, or electronic behavior that a client, former client, student, supervisee, or intern may reasonably interpret as sexually harassing or sexually demeaning.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.148E.220.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Business relationship with client.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A social worker must not purchase goods or services from a client or otherwise engage in a business relationship with a client except when:</p>
